    "english": "This is a fact, for the actual meaning of Master Dharmakirti's statement is as follows. The Numerist School is describing a situation where you are looking at a physical form either from far away, or from up close. They say that, depending on the distance between you and the object, another physical form which is the result of karma, and which stands between your visual consciousness and the original form, is either clear or not. This then determines whether the original form appears to you distinctly or not. It is not the case though, they say, that what determines whether the original form appears clearly or not is whether or not you have a clear impression of this form. In reply then Master Dharmakirti is asking the Numerists: Let's consider these two cases of some intermediate physical form that comes from karma. Do they, or do they not, function to obscure the two original forms, the one at a distance, and the other close by? If they were to obscure them, then your visual consciousness could never see the two original forms, since they would have been obscured by the others."

And it is not our position that there is such a thing as the physical appearance of a person which is at once both attractive and ugly. It is furthermore not our position that the blood and pus represent some kind of physical form which results from karma and grows up between the visual consciousness of the craving spirit and the stream of water. And it is not our position that the craving spirit's eyes see both this blood and pus as some kind of physical form resulting from karma, and the river of water at the same time. It is our position that, by force of his karma, the craving spirit is not able to see the stream of water."

      "english": "This is a fact, for the actual meaning of Master Dharmakirti's statement is as follows. The Numerist School is describing a situation where you are looking at a physical form either from far away, or from up close. They say that, depending on the distance between you and the object, another physical form which is the result of karma, and which stands between your visual consciousness and the original form, is either clear or not. This then determines whether the original form appears to you distinctly or not. It is not the case though, they say, that what determines whether the original form appears clearly or not is whether or not you have a clear impression of this form. In reply then Master Dharmakirti is asking the Numerists: Let's consider these two cases of some intermediate physical form that comes from karma. Do they, or do they not, function to obscure the two original forms, the one at a distance, and the other close by? If they were to obscure them, then your visual consciousness could never see the two original forms, since they would have been obscured by the others."
    },

===== english/277/report.md =====
C05:277 — independent English-first ORIGINAL, PROVISIONAL Codex machine matching; reused English-author/tooling context, no human inspection/certification or semantic approval.

Final: 50 spans; d5=39, d6=8, d7=0, d3=3; nulls=0; proposed errata=0.

The Numerist position is reported for refutation; original versus intervening forms and the final conditional have independent occurrence roles. The shared question obscure is paired once with the second bsgribs; the first is omitted. The third bsgribs is conditional.

Challenge rnam pa shar ba→impression and the distribution of the karmic-form explanation. clear before impression remains unwrapped; skye ba/dbang gis and repeated negative predicates are omitted rather than falsely nulled.

C15:422 matches ACIP/Wylie/English. Physical CRLF source remains exact raw bytes; the separate whitespace-collapse comparison is only a display-field comparison, not lineage or contiguous-byte identity.

Full source/context and original gzip/physical bytes are shared once at ../277/inputs; all61 HGM exact queries (62 full rows) and nine corpus queries (552 full rows) are retained at ../277/evidence, with every raw object checked against the full originals. SQL used URI mode=ro plus query_only=1.

occurrence-declarations.json contains every source occurrence, English order, optional-key status, parent role and specific omission rationale; unwrapped-source-inventory.json preserves both complete complements.

===== tibetan/279/report.md =====
C05:279 Tibetan-first original, primary Codex; PROVISIONAL mechanical proposal only.
Author read its own earlier source preparation and root leads; no current English-original, reconciliation or skeptical findings were read before all three originals froze.
Challenge phyogs snga mas/original position against actual context and the contrary opponent glossary variants; four complete negative assertions versus final positive position; two strict reversed fluids-member pairs; omission of common-locus distributed wording; and final karma/control split. No grammatical correction of final yi dvags kyi is silently made.
Shared evidence in ../277 includes72 complete lexical query sets/71 original master records,15 complete context/raw rows,56 exact preparation replays,9 exact full-field parallel queries and6 original physical extents; complete source/copy/Git records are preserved.
Every intended Tib/English occurrence was compared to the actual unchanged canonical resolver, including all English-order crossings and strict members. Nulls mean documented ellipsis/grammar fusion; uncertain or distributed items are omitted with reasons in the immutable note/items.
One actual canonical invocation for this segment: EXIT=0; stderr=0 bytes; stdout/body=10145 bytes; SHA-256=61722aa823204e3f60e9328b6da9eddf99157134cfc9be56afd9e7a659ba4c42. No failed or superseded current attempt; no post-generation spec/note edit.
Spans=64; nulls=3; nonnull D5=43. Exact optional fields/occurrences remain in spec.json and intended-spans.json.
Errata=0. Final YI DVAGS KYI is preserved exactly in ACIP/derived Wylie and in C15:424. Its English subject role is recorded, but grammar alone without a verified correction does not authorize an erratum or source substitution; the genitive/agentive question remains a skeptical-review challenge. Full five-ground decision is in errata-analysis.json.
Source/master pins and baseline are unchanged. HGM glosses retain their original tier and fields; reference/auto-aligned anomalies are not used to compose English or promote machine evidence.
Independent reconciliation and skeptical semantic acceptance are still required. No native, phone, MAIN, paid API, database write or source modification was performed.

And it is not our position that there is such a thing as the physical appearance of a person which is at once both attractive and ugly. It is furthermore not our position that the blood and pus represent some kind of physical form which results from karma and grows up between the visual consciousness of the craving spirit and the stream of water. And it is not our position that the craving spirit's eyes see both this blood and pus as some kind of physical form resulting from karma, and the river of water at the same time.
